## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a keynote built for a large room, and it works as one: short claim titles, one large chart or photo per slide, and a consistent grey panel and blue accent system that makes the chart pages easy to read at a glance. The route moves from rebuttal to sustainability, products, charging, energy, and then to the ecosystem diagram (slides 23 and 24) and AI, so the 'more than a car company' case arrives in a sensible order. Several chart pages have real weaknesses, though: slide 8 stacks six years of thin bars per company, so the ranking is hard to read and the 2023 emphasis is faint, slides 3, 6 and 7 use tiny axis and legend text, and slide 5 gives a mostly empty 0 to 100% axis with a pale subtitle that carries the key 85% figure. Many full-bleed photo slides (10 to 15, 18, 19, 26 to 29) carry only a title over busy images with weak contrast in places, and slides 31 to 33 repeat or end flatly.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the short claim-style titles, the consistent grey chart panel with a single blue accent, and the stepwise ecosystem diagram on slides 23 and 24 with its clear labels and arrows.

**Most useful repair:** Make the key figure on each chart slide readable by enlarging axis labels and legends and moving the 85% claim on slide 5 out of the pale subtitle into a callout, and simplify slide 8 to show 2023 bars with a labeled Tesla value.

**What would change the score:** I would add a protective gradient or darker overlay under titles on the photo slides, trim the chart y-axis range on slide 5, and drop the duplicate closing slides.

_Confidence high. 33 of 33 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is the keynote from Tesla's 2024 annual shareholder meeting. It opens with a slide rebutting common market criticisms, then moves through sections on sustainability and manufacturing, battery durability, worker safety, total cost of ownership, vehicle production and product launches, charging infrastructure, energy storage and energy gross profit, services, and AI. Its central argument is that Tesla is more than a car company, with the AI compute and robotics slides leading toward its Robotaxi and Optimus efforts. It closes with a mission slide and a call to sign up for future events. Slides worth studying include the Model 3/Y battery retention chart (slide 5), which pairs a single data curve with a clear headline, the production comparison bar chart (slide 8), which ranks competitors visually, and the ecosystem diagram (slides 23 and 24), which builds the product map step by step with an added layer for the AI roadmap. Many slides use short headline-style titles and a large chart or image, with footnotes for sources.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides are in the Tesla shareholder meeting deck?**

The Tesla deck has 33 slides. It is the keynote from Tesla's 2024 annual shareholder meeting.

**What does the Tesla deck say about battery durability?**

Slide 5 says Model 3 and Model Y battery packs retain 85% of their capacity on average after 200K miles of driving. The slide's chart shows Model 3/Y Long Range battery capacity retention per distance traveled.

**What does the Tesla deck say about energy storage deployments and gross profit?**

Slide 20 says Tesla has more than 40GWh of capacity between its Lathrop Megafactory and Gigafactory Nevada. Slide 21 is titled "We Generated Record Gross Profit from Energy in 2023" and attributes it to ramping Megapack volumes.

**What does the Tesla deck say about EV production rankings in 2023?**

Slide 8 is titled "We Produced the Most EVs Globally in 2023" and shows a bar chart of Electric Vehicles Produced from 2018 to 2023 for 11 manufacturers. The slide's source note says Tesla data are production volumes, and the source is EV-volumes.com.

**Text**

ANNUAL / SHAREHOLDER / MEETING / CYBER / ROUNDUP / 2024 / TEXAS

**Layout**

Single centred composition on a dark textured wall. The lava-textured title sits in the upper-middle, stacked in three centred lines, with the neon Texas logo centred directly below it. No footer, slide number, or margin elements; the content sits in the central third and the rest of the frame is empty wall.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark textured concrete-like wall backdrop with faint vertical and horizontal panel seam lines near one-third and two-thirds widths. Above centre, the title 'ANNUAL SHAREHOLDER MEETING' is rendered in a heavy, wide, all-caps display face whose letters look like glowing embers and cracked lava, orange-red with dark fissures, with small orange sparks and faint grey smoke rising behind it, the lines slightly tilted upward to the right. Below, a neon sign in the outline of the State of Texas: a blue neon outline (about #2FA8E0) of the state shape, with 'CYBER ROUNDUP' in red neon (about #E23B3B) in a squared techno font on two lines, and below it '2024' in orange neon (about #F39A2E) inside a teal neon rounded-rectangle capsule (about #2CC7C0) with 'TEXAS' in small spaced orange capitals beneath the numerals. The blue outline runs around the whole state shape and its panhandle at top and a tail at bottom-right. Lighting is dramatic and low-key, with the neon glowing against the dark wall; composition is centred and symmetrical about the vertical axis.

**Design**

Background: Dark slate-teal textured wall photograph (about #1B2A31) with faint panel seams. Dark slate-teal wall background (about #1B2A31) with subtle grain and vignetting. Title letters in a heavy wide sans-serif display with ember-orange (about #E0622A) and red-brown fill, high contrast. Neon elements use red #E23B3B, blue #2FA8E0, teal #2CC7C0 and orange #F39A2E with soft glow. Title text roughly 8% of slide height per line; neon 'CYBER ROUNDUP' about 9% cap height; '2024' about 4%; 'TEXAS' about 1.6%, spaced capitals in a squared techno sans.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Setting a New Standard for Sustainable Manufacturing
Water usage per vehicle produced down almost 25% since 2018—well below the industry average of 3.37 m³
35% less energy used per vehicle produced in Shanghai vs. Fremont
100% of Gigafactory Berlin-Brandenburg's electricity usage matched with renewables
T E S L A
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left in a single line across roughly the top 14% of the slide, left edge aligned to a 4.4% margin. Below it, three equal-width tall photo cards sit side by side in one row (about 28.6% wide each, 70.1% tall, starting at y 19.4%) with gutters of about 2.7% and a right margin of about 4.4%. Each caption is bottom-left inside its card, left-aligned at about 2.9% inset from the card edge. A footer row at y 94% holds the Tesla wordmark at left and the copyright line beside it. No page number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three photographs, each in a rounded-corner box with a dark gradient fading from transparent at the upper part to near-black at the bottom to support the white caption. Left photo: a white/light-grey car body shell inside a spray-wash booth with blue-tipped nozzles on overhead pipes and side rails, spray mist in the air; grey and black-and-white tones with a white-grey body; vertical framing, full card, not cropped by the box; bottom of the card darkened. Middle photo: a Tesla body-in-white on an assembly fixture surrounded by red KUKA industrial robot arms with black and green cables and silver grippers, with silver-grey car body panels and grey mylar-wrapped components; dominant colours red, silver and grey; bright factory lighting; centred symmetrical framing; full card; bottom darkened. Right photo: a man in a black Tesla sweatshirt with a black cap, reaching with one arm onto a silver-grey car body shell on a line, long factory aisle with rows of overhead linear lights receding into the background; cool white and grey tones with black clothing; full card; bottom darkened. No logos other than the small Tesla wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title is in Universal Sans Display Medium, black #000000, sentence-case, tight tracking, about 5.9% of slide height. Body captions are Universal Sans Text Light in white #FFFFFF, about 2.2% of slide height, with key figures set in Universal Sans Text Medium (bold-ish) in white. Copyright line is Universal Sans Display Regular in grey #ACACB0, about 1.3% of slide height. Photo cards have rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and no border or shadow. Accent colour comes only from the red robot arms in the middle photo.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A single retention curve with a headline stating the takeaway makes the data read at a glance.
- Review: Several chart pages have real weaknesses, though: slide 8 stacks six years of thin bars per company, so the ranking is hard to read and the 2023 emphasis is faint, slides 3, 6 and 7 use tiny axis and legend text, and slide 5 gives a mostly empty 0 to 100% axis with a pale subtitle that carries the key 85% figure.
- Suggested repair: Make the key figure on each chart slide readable by enlarging axis labels and legends and moving the 85% claim on slide 5 out of the pale subtitle into a callout, and simplify slide 8 to show 2023 bars with a labeled Tesla value.
- What would change the score: I would add a protective gradient or darker overlay under titles on the photo slides, trim the chart y-axis range on slide 5, and drop the duplicate closing slides.

**Text**

Our Batteries Are Designed to Outlast Our Vehicles
Model 3 and Model Y battery packs retain 85% of their capacity on average after 200K miles of driving
Model 3/Y Long Range
Battery Capacity Retention per Distance Traveled

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a near-white slide with a left margin of about 4.4% of slide width. The title sits top-left at about 8% of slide height, with a light grey subtitle directly beneath it. A large rounded chart panel spans the full content width from about 19% to 90% of slide height, with the chart title at top-left and legend at top-right inside the panel. The Tesla wordmark and copyright line sit in a footer row at about 94% of slide height; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One line chart inside the panel. Type: retention line with a shaded standard-deviation band. Chart title: 'Model 3/Y Long Range'. Subtitle: 'Battery Capacity Retention per Distance Traveled'. Y axis: retention in percent, labelled 0%, 50%, 80% and 100%, with the scale running 0% to about 110%. X axis: distance traveled, labelled 0, 50K, 100K, 150K and 200K Miles, running from 0 to about 210K miles. Horizontal gridlines are thin mid-grey lines at 100%, 80%, 50% and 0%. Retention series (dark blue line, about #3B63DB): starts at 100% at 0 miles, falls steeply to about 95% by 10K miles, reaches about 90% near 50K miles, about 87% near 100K miles, about 85% near 150K miles, and ends at about 84% near 200K miles. Standard deviation band (light blue fill, about #B3C6F7): a band of roughly ±3 to ±5 points around the retention line, widening slightly after 150K miles and dipping to about 78% near 200K miles. A thin grey outline near the origin rises to about 108% at 0 miles and tapers to meet the 100% line by about 20K miles. No values are printed on the chart, so all values are estimates marked 'about'. Legend (top-right, horizontal): a dark blue filled dot followed by 'Retention', then a light blue filled dot followed by 'Standard Deviation'. No points are highlighted. The Tesla wordmark is a small grey letter-spaced logo at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background is off-white #FEFEFE. The chart panel is a very light grey #F6F7F9 rounded rectangle with a corner radius of about 1% of slide width and no visible border or shadow. Title text is black #000000, the chart title is near-black #202024, and the subtitle is light grey #989898. Axis labels and legend text are mid-grey #8D8D8D. The copyright line is pale grey #ACACB0. Accent colours are dark blue #3B63DB for the retention line and light blue #B3C6F7 for the deviation band. Typefaces are a geometric grotesque sans-serif in the Universal Sans family: Medium for the title, Light for the subtitle, Display Medium for the chart title, and Display Regular for labels. Title is about 3.7% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, chart title about 2.2%, and labels about 2.0%, with the copyright about 1.3%. Gridlines are thin mid-grey #B0B0B0 lines, about 0.1% of slide height thick. Shapes are flat, with no gradients or shadows.

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout on a near-white background with a left margin of about 4.4% of slide width. The title sits top-left at about 8.3% from the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it. A large light-grey rounded panel spans about 4.4% to 95.5% horizontally and 19.4% to 89.5% vertically, holding a bold caption at its top-left and two side-by-side charts (bar chart left, line chart right) that share the same baseline. A row of Tesla wordmark letters and a small copyright line sit at the bottom-left footer at about 94% from the top. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Two charts inside the grey panel. Left chart: vertical bar chart titled Work-Related Injury Rate, y-axis from 0 to 7 (unitless injury rate, gridlines at every integer), x-categories 2021, 2022, 2023 and Industry Average. Series values: 2021 about 3.55, 2022 about 2.85, 2023 about 2.5, Industry Average about 6.95; no values are printed on the slide. Bars are solid royal blue (about #4169E1) with rounded top corners for 2021, 2022 and 2023, and solid mid-grey (about #949494) with rounded corners for Industry Average. No legend and no highlighted points. Right chart: line chart titled Improvement Suggestions, y-axis from 0 to 700k with gridlines every 100k, x-categories 2021, 2022, 2023. One pale periwinkle (about #B3C6F5) line with rounded joins and round caps, about 0.6% of slide height thick, rising from about 50k in 2021 to about 330k in 2022 and about 660k in 2023; the values are estimates marked about since none are printed as points. No legend and no markers. Gridlines are thin light grey (about #D9D9D9) horizontal lines, 0.2% of slide height thick, across both charts. Footer: a row of five grey Tesla wordmark letters (T, E, S, L, A-style glyphs) at the bottom left, about #9A9A9A, with the copyright text beside them. No photographs or icons apart from the wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid near-white #FEFEFE, covering the whole slide. Clean corporate minimalist look on a near-white ground. Background near-white #FEFEFE. Main title in black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSansText Medium), weight medium, about 3.7% of slide height for the title. Subtitle in light grey #989898, UniversalSansText Light, about 2.55% of slide height. Chart caption in dark grey #202024, UniversalSansDisplay Medium, about 2.2%. Chart titles and axis labels in mid grey #8D8D8D, UniversalSansDisplay Regular, about 2.04%. Copyright in light grey #ACACB0, about 1.3%. Accents: royal blue #4169E1 bars, grey #949494 benchmark bar, pale periwinkle #B3C6F5 line. The grey panel is a very light cool grey (about #F5F6F8) with softly rounded corners (about 1% of slide width radius) and no visible border or shadow. Gridlines are thin and light grey with no dashes.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Review: Several chart pages have real weaknesses, though: slide 8 stacks six years of thin bars per company, so the ranking is hard to read and the 2023 emphasis is faint, slides 3, 6 and 7 use tiny axis and legend text, and slide 5 gives a mostly empty 0 to 100% axis with a pale subtitle that carries the key 85% figure.

**Text**

We’re Making EVs Affordable
Total Cost of Ownership per Mile
$0.00
$0.20
$0.40
$0.60
$0.80
$1.00
$1.20
Model Y
BMW X3
Depreciation, Taxes, Fees, Incentives & Financing
Insurance
Maintenance, Tires & Repairs
Fuel (Electricity or Gas)
Copyright 2023 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white field with the title at top left, starting about 4.4% from the left edge and sitting at about 8.4% from the top. A large light-grey rounded panel spans from about 4.5% to 95.5% horizontally and from about 19.4% to 89.5% vertically, holding the chart title at top left, the dollar axis labels across the top, two horizontal bars with row labels on the left, and the legend along the bottom of the panel. The footer sits at the bottom left, with the Tesla wordmark at about 4.4% from the left and the copyright line to its right, both near 94-96% from the top. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Horizontal stacked bar chart titled 'Total Cost of Ownership per Mile'. The horizontal axis runs along the top from $0.00 to $1.20 in $0.20 steps, with unit US dollars per mile; the printed labels are $0.00, $0.20, $0.40, $0.60, $0.80, $1.00 and $1.20, and thin light-grey vertical gridlines drop from each label, with the scale extending slightly past $1.20 to about $1.25. There is no vertical axis title; the two categories are labelled on the left as Model Y (top bar) and BMW X3 (bottom bar). Series, all stacked from $0.00: Depreciation, Taxes, Fees, Incentives & Financing; Insurance; Maintenance, Tires & Repairs; Fuel (Electricity or Gas). Model Y segments, read from the bar against the gridlines (values are estimates, no values printed): depreciation about $0.46, insurance about $0.12 (ending near $0.58), maintenance about $0.07 (ending near $0.65), fuel about $0.05 (total about $0.70). BMW X3 segments (estimates, no values printed): depreciation about $0.76, insurance about $0.09 (ending near $0.85), maintenance about $0.16 (ending near $1.01), fuel about $0.16 (total about $1.17). No points are highlighted. Legend at the bottom in one row with round colour dots: Depreciation dot blue, Insurance dot mid blue, Maintenance dot light blue, Fuel dot pale blue. The Model Y bar uses blue shades (depreciation #3D6AE1, insurance #6A8CEA, maintenance #8FA6EF, fuel #B4C5F4) and the BMW X3 bar uses grey shades (depreciation #777777, insurance #999999, maintenance #BBBBBB, fuel #CFCFCF). Bars are thin rounded-end bars about 3.7% of slide height, with the bar ends rounded.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a light grey #F6F7F9 rounded panel inside. Clean minimal corporate look on white. Background white #FFFFFF; the chart panel is very light grey #F6F7F9 with about 1% corner radius and no shadow or border. Title text is black #000000, in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSansDisplay Medium), about 5.9% of slide height. Chart title is black #000000 in medium weight at about 2.0% of slide height. Axis labels, row labels and legend text are mid grey #8D8D8D in regular weight at about 1.85% of slide height. Footer copyright is light grey #ACACB0 at about 1.3% of slide height. Gridlines are light grey #D0D0D0, about 0.2% of width thick, running from the axis labels down to the baseline. Accent colours are the blue and grey bar series. No gradients.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A ranked bar chart of EV production by manufacturer, with the source noted beneath, supports a clear claim.
- Review: Several chart pages have real weaknesses, though: slide 8 stacks six years of thin bars per company, so the ranking is hard to read and the 2023 emphasis is faint, slides 3, 6 and 7 use tiny axis and legend text, and slide 5 gives a mostly empty 0 to 100% axis with a pale subtitle that carries the key 85% figure.
- Suggested repair: Make the key figure on each chart slide readable by enlarging axis labels and legends and moving the 85% claim on slide 5 out of the pale subtitle into a callout, and simplify slide 8 to show 2023 bars with a labeled Tesla value.

**Text**

We Produced the Most EVs Globally in 2023
Electric Vehicles Produced
2018
2019
2020

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 8.4% from the top with its left edge at about 4.4% of the slide width. A large light-grey rounded panel fills the lower area from about 19.4% to 89.5% of the height and from about 4.5% to 95.6% of the width, holding the chart title and legend at the top, the horizontal bar chart in the middle, the axis labels below it and the source line at the bottom. The Tesla wordmark logo and the copyright line sit in the footer at about 94.3% from the top, left-aligned at about 4.4% of the width. There is no visible slide number.

**Visuals**

One horizontal grouped bar chart titled 'Electric Vehicles Produced' with the x-axis labelled 0, 400K, 800K, 1.2M, 1.6M, 2M (units: vehicles produced, range 0 to 2 million, linear, gridlines absent). The y-axis lists 11 manufacturers top to bottom: Tesla, BYD, VW Group, GAC, Hyundai Motor, GM, BMW Group, Geely Auto Group, R-N-M Alliance, SAIC, Stellantis. Each manufacturer has six thin horizontal bars, one per year 2018 (top, palest) to 2023 (bottom, darkest). Tesla's bars are blue, from pale blue-grey for 2018 to saturated blue for 2023; the other manufacturers' bars are grey-to-black. Legend: six dots with year labels, 2018 pale lavender-grey, 2019 light periwinkle, 2020 periwinkle, 2021 medium blue-violet, 2022 blue, 2023 strong blue, arranged in one row at the top right of the panel. Values printed on the chart: none; the 2023 estimates read from bar lengths are: Tesla about 1.86M (highest, bar reaches about 1.86M), BYD about 1.59M, VW Group about 0.76M, GAC about 0.49M, Hyundai Motor about 0.42M, GM about 0.38M, BMW Group about 0.38M, Geely Auto Group about 0.31M, R-N-M Alliance about 0.27M, SAIC about 0.26M, Stellantis about 0.26M. Earlier-year bars are estimates only: Tesla 2018 about 0.25M, 2019 about 0.37M, 2020 about 0.51M, 2021 about 0.94M, 2022 about 1.38M. No highlighted points beyond the longest Tesla bar. Logo: the Tesla wordmark rendered as five thin grey letter glyphs in a row (T, E, S, L, A shapes, grey #A5A5A5), bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal corporate look on white. Panel is a very light grey rounded rectangle with a barely visible edge and no shadow, corner radius about 1% of the slide width. Title is near-black #000000 in UniversalSansDisplay Medium (geometric grotesque sans-serif, medium weight), about 5.9% of slide height, tight letter spacing. Chart title is #000000 in UniversalSansDisplay Medium at about 2.2% of height. Axis and category labels are dark grey #2E2E2E in UniversalSansDisplay Regular at about 2.0% of height. Axis value labels and legend text are mid-grey #8D8D8D at about 2.0% of height. Source line is light grey #A5A5A5 at about 1.85% of height. Copyright line is #ACACB0 at about 1.3% of height. Bars are thin solid lines about 0.6% of slide height thick with square ends, no outlines. Accent colour is blue #3E5FD6 for Tesla's 2022 and 2023 bars.

### Slide 9

**Text**

And Over 6 Million Vehicles All-Time
CONGRATULATIONS!
TESLA GLOBAL 6,000,000 VEHICLES
6
DOORLINE
VOL FAULT
EXIT
EXIT
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Single-column full-width layout on a white field: the title sits top-left at about 8% from the top with a 4.4% left margin, and a wide rounded-corner photograph spans the full content width below it. A small grey Tesla wordmark sits bottom-left in the footer with the copyright line beside it; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a large group of roughly 300 Tesla factory employees posed in an indoor production hall, most wearing black jackets, hi-vis safety vests (red, yellow and grey) and caps. In the centre foreground a large gold foil balloon in the shape of the numeral 6 stands beside a dark grey Tesla Model 3 whose bonnet carries a white paper banner reading CONGRATULATIONS! / TESLA GLOBAL 6,000,000 VEHICLES. Several workers in front kneel or crouch; some raise arms in a heart or cheering pose. Background shows white industrial ceiling, structural white columns, red overhead pipes, green EXIT signs, production line equipment and a yellow DOORLINE VOL FAULT sign. Wide panoramic framing shot from slightly elevated front angle, even overhead fluorescent lighting, dominant colours white, grey, black, hi-vis yellow and gold. The photo is boxed with rounded corners, not full-bleed, and is not cropped at its visible edges.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 10

**Text**

In 2023, We Broke Our Yearly Production Record at Fremont
TESLA
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with a single line headline set across the top of the slide, left-aligned at about 4.4% from the left edge, sitting in the upper band of the image. A faint TESLA wordmark sits at bottom left and a small copyright line sits beside it at bottom left-centre, with no page number and no other footer.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of an automotive factory interior, wide one-point perspective looking down a long aisle. In the foreground left, two silver-white aluminium car body shells (unpainted sheet metal, one with a rounded roofline) sit on white steel A-frame racks on a flat pallet base with red and white safety markings. In the centre-right, a third body shell is lifted on a white flatbed rack carried by a red electric forklift whose operator is partly visible. Background right shows red industrial robot arms and another red forklift with a driver in a row of production cells. Ceiling of exposed white steel trusses with rows of fluorescent lights; white and grey concrete floor with a white painted lane line running toward the viewer on the right. Lighting is flat, cool and even from overhead fluorescents. Dominant colours are silver, white, light grey and red accents. Cropped edge to edge; no border. A dark gradient overlay covers the top 20.2% of the slide to support the headline. A small red fire-safety sign with a white pictogram sits just right of the headline end.

**Design**

Background is black #000000 beneath a full-bleed photograph, with a dark translucent gradient across the top band (fading from dark at the top edge to transparent). Headline in white #FFFFFF. Footer copyright in light grey #ACACB0. Faint TESLA wordmark in pale grey at low opacity. Headline typeface is UniversalSansDisplay Medium, a geometric grotesque sans-serif, medium weight, sentence case, about 5.9% of slide height for the headline (cap height about 4.1%). Footer is UniversalSansDisplay Regular, about 1.3% of slide height. No rules, no rounded shapes, no shadows on the text. Accent colour is the red of the forklifts and the fire sign, about #D8352A.

**Visuals**

One aerial photograph filling the entire slide. Surrounding the factory: a large parking lot full of parked vehicles at upper left, a smaller multi-storey grey office block near centre-top, a grey water tower and a white industrial structure with steam at upper right, a pond and tree lines in the background, and a multi-lane highway in the foreground with cars and a white semi-trailer travelling along it. A white building and a dense car park appear at the bottom right corner. Composition: wide, high-angle, slightly oblique view from the south-west, with the factory running horizontally across the middle third. Lighting: diffuse overcast daylight with soft haze on the distant horizon. Dominant colours: light grey and off-white roofs, dark green and grey tree cover, muted beige dirt ground, dark navy solar panels. The photograph is full-bleed, unboxed, uncropped, with no tint except a dark gradient darkening the top band behind the title.

### Slide 17

**Text**

Our Supercharger Network is Growing
~58K Superchargers
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark map fills the whole slide. The title sits top-left at about 4.4% from the left edge and 8.4% from the top, left-aligned on one line. The map label sits centred horizontally, low on the slide, over Africa. The Tesla wordmark and copyright line form a footer row at the bottom left, both on the same baseline near 95% of the slide height. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One world map illustration in a flat, slightly embossed style. Landmasses are charcoal with thin darker country borders and a soft drop shadow along their lower-left edges, so they appear raised. Red glowing dots mark charging sites. Dense clusters sit in the contiguous United States, especially the West Coast, Texas and the Eastern Seaboard. They also appear in western and central Europe and the UK, around the Gulf and Israel, across eastern China, in South Korea, Japan, Taiwan, Thailand and Malaysia, along the south-east coast of Australia, and in New Zealand. Sparse dots spread across Canada, Mexico, Russia and Scandinavia. Africa, South America and most of Asia have few or no dots. The map is viewed straight on with no crop. No photographs or other icons appear. Dominant colours are charcoal #2B2B2D for the map, with red dots near #E8413A glowing through a soft halo. A dark gradient band runs across the top 20% of the slide, fading from near-black #000000 at the top to transparent. The map label is pale pink-white with a red glow.

**Design**

Background: Dark charcoal #2B2B2D with a near-black #000000 gradient band at the top, over which the world map illustration sits.

### Slide 18

**Text**

Expanding Charging Access for All
T E S L A
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the slide edge to edge with the title in the upper-left at about 4.4% from the left edge and 8.4% from the top, on top of the photo. The Tesla wordmark sits bottom-left at about 4.4% from the left and 94.3% from the top, with the copyright line immediately to its right at about 16.9% from the left on the same baseline band. There is no slide number and no footer beyond the copyright line.

**Visuals**

One photograph covering the whole slide. Subject: a dark, glossy, low-slung electric sedan in the foreground, shown in side profile and cut off by the bottom and right edges, parked under a solar-panel carport. Action: the car is stopped beside a row of Tesla Supercharger stalls. The carport is a grid of white steel beams and columns supporting dark blue-black solar panels seen from below. Behind the car on the right are Tesla charging posts with red Tesla lettering and red charging handles, a parked white vehicle, palm trees and a low building under a pale sky. A white 'Ford' oval logo and small text reading 'Now open 7 days a week' are printed on the car's side window, and a faint sign reading 'Lincoln' is reflected in the glass. Composition: wide, low camera angle, horizontal split with the car in the lower half and the carport roof in the upper half. Lighting: bright daylight with soft shadows under the canopy. Dominant colours: black and deep navy for the car and panels, white for the beams, pale sky blue and grey-white highlights. The photo is full-bleed and uncropped apart from the car running off the edges, with no border or frame. A dark translucent band overlays the top 20.2% of the slide behind the title. The Tesla wordmark is a small light-grey logo in the lower left, rendered as widely spaced capital letters.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of a solar carport and electric car covering the entire slide. Black background with the photograph covering it. Title in white UniversalSansDisplay Medium, sans-serif geometric grotesque, roughly 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Copyright line in grey UniversalSansDisplay Regular, about 1.3% of slide height. Accent colours: none beyond the photo. No shapes, rules or corner radii. No shadows or gradients other than the dark band at the top of the photo.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Innovating Battery Manufacturing
Tesla Lithium Refinery
TESLA
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed aerial photograph with the title placed top-left over a dark gradient band, a centered caption in the lower middle, and the TESLA wordmark and copyright line along the bottom edge. Left margin is about 4.4% of slide width; there is no grid, column structure, footer bar or slide number.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed aerial drone photograph of a battery and lithium refinery construction site in flat rural terrain. Subject: a large white arched-roof hangar-style building at centre right with a smaller grey arched shelter beside it; several rows of white modular buildings with rooftop solar-type panels at lower left; a large graded dirt pad in the centre with a foundation outline; a steel-frame structure with a white-roofed plant and tanks, pipe racks and cranes at upper centre and upper left; many parked cars and pickup trucks in a lot at lower centre and lower right; stacked materials and white containers at right; and a white dirt road network running diagonally across the frame. Bare brown earth, green irrigated fields and a dark horizon line are visible at the top. Composition is a high oblique aerial view from the south-east, framed wide with the site occupying most of the frame. Lighting is bright, even daylight with soft shadows. Dominant colours are beige-brown earth, white structures, green farmland and grey-blue steel. The photograph is full-bleed and not cropped, with no tint except the darkened gradient band at the top. Overlay: a dark grey gradient band across the top 20.2% of the slide fading to transparent, which sits behind the title. Logo: a light grey TESLA wordmark in widely letter-spaced capitals at lower left.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed aerial photograph of the site, with the solid #000000 fill behind it. Full-bleed photographic slide with no decorative frame. Title white (#FFFFFF) in a medium-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSansDisplay Medium), approximately 5.9% of slide height. Caption white (#FFFFFF) in the same family, medium weight, about 2.8% of slide height. Copyright line in light grey (#ACACB0), regular weight, 1.3% of slide height. Wordmark in light grey-white with wide tracking. Accent colour: none. Lines and shapes: none drawn; the top gradient is a soft fade with no hard edge. No corner radii, shadows or borders.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart, untitled, with eight bars. Y axis: 0 GWh to 40 GWh, linear, gridlines and labels every 5 GWh. X axis: categories 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023 and Megafactory Capacity. No axis titles or units beyond the GWh labels. Values are not printed on the bars; the values below are estimates from bar heights: 2018 about 1 GWh, 2019 about 2 GWh, 2020 about 3 GWh, 2021 about 4 GWh, 2022 about 6 GWh, 2023 about 15 GWh, Megafactory Capacity about 40 GWh. Series is one, royal blue bars in #4068E0 with rounded top corners; the Megafactory Capacity bar is light periwinkle #B8C8FF and is the highlighted bar. No legend. Gridlines are light grey #D9D9D9, about 0.2% of slide height thick, at 0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35 and 40 GWh. Bars are about 2.1% of slide width wide. Also present: a small grey Tesla wordmark logo in the footer, letters T E S L A with the Tesla T-mark style, colour about #ACACB0, at x 4.4% y 94.3%, measured image box 29.6% by 1.8%, visible glyphs about 8.4% wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F6F7F9 rounded panel behind the chart. Clean minimal corporate style. White background #FFFFFF, no gradients or shadows. Chart panel fill #F6F7F9 with a small corner radius of about 1% of slide width and no border. Title in UniversalSansText Medium, a grotesque sans-serif, weight medium, black #000000, size 3.7% of slide height for the cap-height-inclusive line, sentence case. Subtitle in UniversalSansText Light, sans-serif, light weight, grey #989898, size 2.55% of slide height. Axis labels and category labels in UniversalSansDisplay Regular, sans-serif, regular weight, grey #8D8D8D, size 2.04% of slide height. Copyright in UniversalSansDisplay Regular, #ACACB0, size 1.3% of slide height. Body text is therefore small relative to the title. Accents: royal blue #4068E0 bars, periwinkle #B8C8FF for the highlighted bar. Lines are hairline light grey gridlines, no axis line beyond the 0 GWh baseline.

Single-column chart slide on a white field. The title sits top-left at about 8% of slide height with a light grey subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned to x 4.4%. The Tesla wordmark and copyright line sit in the footer at y about 94%, with no page number and no slide number.

Bar chart titled by the slide headline, showing annual gross profit from Energy in US dollars billions. Y-axis runs from -$0.4B to $1.6B in steps of $0.4B, labelled $1.6B, $1.2B, $0.8B, $0.4B, $0.0B and -$0.4B, with horizontal light-grey gridlines at each step. **Layout**

Left-aligned title block at the top with a bold one-line headline and a light grey subtitle beneath it, both starting at about 4.4% from the left edge. A single full-width rounded light grey chart panel fills the middle of the slide between about 4.5% and 95.6% horizontally, with the chart's y-axis labels at its left, six year labels evenly spaced along its bottom, and a small footer row at bottom-left holding the grey wordmark and the copyright line. No page number is shown.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, titled by the slide headline. Y-axis (labelled $0.6B, $0.4B, $0.2B, $0.0B, -$0.2B, -$0.4B, -$0.6B) runs from -$0.6B to $0.6B in $0.2B steps, in US dollars billions; no axis title is printed. X-axis shows years 2018 to 2023 with no axis title. Each year has one blue bar starting at the $0.0B zero line. Series are not labelled with numeric values; values are estimated: 2018 about -$0.47B; 2019 about -$0.52B; 2020 about -$0.36B; 2021 about -$0.10B; 2022 about +$0.22B; 2023 about +$0.49B. No legend. No highlighted points; all six bars are the same blue, with rounded ends and no data labels. Gridlines are seven thin light grey horizontal lines, one at each axis label, running from the y-axis to the right edge of the plot area. Bars are about 4.9% of slide width and are placed at x 14.2, 28.2, 42.2, 56.2, 70.2 and 84.2 percent respectively, in year order.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal corporate look on white. Title in black (#000000) medium-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSans Text Medium), about 3.7% of slide height with capitals about 2.6% tall. Subtitle in light grey (#989898) light-weight sans-serif (UniversalSans Text Light), about 2.55% of slide height. Bars in royal blue (#4169E1) with rounded corners of roughly 4 px radius, no shadow. Gridlines light grey (#CCCCCC), about 2 px thick, flat. Chart panel fill very light grey (#F6F7F9) with rounded corners of about 12 px radius and no border or shadow. Footer copyright in light grey (#ACACB0) sans-serif, about 1.3% of slide height. Wordmark in grey (#9A9A9A) widely letter-spaced. No gradients.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Review: The route moves from rebuttal to sustainability, products, charging, energy, and then to the ecosystem diagram (slides 23 and 24) and AI, so the 'more than a car company' case arrives in a sensible order.
- Worth keeping: Keep the short claim-style titles, the consistent grey chart panel with a single blue accent, and the stepwise ecosystem diagram on slides 23 and 24 with its clear labels and arrows.

**Text**

Tesla is Way More Than a Car Company
Autobidder
Full Self-Driving
Solar
Energy Storage
Charging
Vehicles
Insurance
Lithium Refining
Battery Cells
Service & Collision
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

A single-column title sits top-left at about 8% from the top, left-aligned at 4.4% from the left edge. Below it, a large rounded light-grey panel spans the slide width (4.5% to 95.6% horizontally, 19.4% to 89.5% vertically) holding a hand-placed flow diagram of nine icon-and-label nodes on an irregular grid: left column Solar and Autobidder, middle nodes Energy Storage, Charging, Lithium Refining and Battery Cells, right column Full Self-Driving, Vehicles, Insurance and Service & Collision. A grey Tesla wordmark sits bottom-left at about 94.3% from the top, with the copyright line beside it; there is no slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram with nine nodes, each a small product image above a centred label, connected by thin grey arrows. Autobidder: a silver desktop monitor showing a dark screen with a purple-and-white waveform graph; a double-headed diagonal arrow runs from it down-right to Energy Storage. Solar: a tall dark charcoal-grey solar panel, portrait; a right-pointing arrow runs horizontally to Energy Storage. Energy Storage: a white-silver Powerwall wall battery, portrait, with a rounded top; a right-pointing arrow runs to Charging, and a diagonal arrow from Battery Cells points up-left into it. Charging: a white and black Tesla wall-charger/Supercharger post with a red small logo near the top and a black cable; a right-pointing arrow runs to Vehicles. Vehicles: a red Model Y crossover in side view, glossy, on a white background; arrows arrive from Charging (left), Insurance (right), Full Self-Driving (above, double-headed) and Service & Collision (below), and a diagonal arrow from Battery Cells points up-right into it. Insurance: a black-framed smartphone showing an app screen with a blue gauge reading 90 and a blue button; a left-pointing arrow runs to Vehicles. Full Self-Driving: a top-down illustration of a white car on a light-grey road with lane lines and a blue forward sensor beam, faded at the edges; a vertical double-headed arrow connects it to Vehicles. Service & Collision: a dark grey Model Y in side view with two people in dark clothing beside it; an upward arrow runs from it to Vehicles. Lithium Refining: a grey industrial refining plant on a flat base with two tall stacks; a right-pointing arrow runs to Battery Cells. Battery Cells: a single silver-grey cylindrical battery cell, upright; arrows leave it up-left to Energy Storage and up-right to Vehicles. Diagonal and horizontal arrows are light grey with small open arrowheads. Images are cut out on the grey panel with no boxes or frames. No photographs fill the slide and there are no tints or overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide, with a light grey #F5F6F8 rounded panel behind the diagram. Flat, minimal layout on white with a single very light grey panel. Title in a heavy geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSans Display Medium, close to Helvetica Neue Medium), black #000000, large (about 5.9% of slide height, roughly 8 percent cap height proportion). Node labels in a regular-weight grotesque sans (UniversalSans Display Regular), near-black #111111, about 1.85% of slide height. Copyright line in the same family, light grey #ACACB0, about 1.3% of slide height. Panel fill very light grey #F5F6F8 with rounded corners of roughly 0.8% of slide width, no border, no shadow. Connector lines thin mid-grey (about #A8A8AE, roughly 1 px) with small open arrowheads. Tesla wordmark in light grey (about #B5B5BA) with widely spaced letters. No gradients.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Building on the earlier ecosystem diagram with added Robotaxi, AI Compute and Optimus layers shows the roadmap as an extension of the existing picture.
- Review: The route moves from rebuttal to sustainability, products, charging, energy, and then to the ecosystem diagram (slides 23 and 24) and AI, so the 'more than a car company' case arrives in a sensible order.
- Worth keeping: Keep the short claim-style titles, the consistent grey chart panel with a single blue accent, and the stepwise ecosystem diagram on slides 23 and 24 with its clear labels and arrows.

**Text**

We’re Also the Leader in Real-World AI
Autobidder
Optimus
AI Compute
Full Self-Driving
Robotaxi
Solar
Energy Storage
Charging
Vehicles
Insurance
Lithium Refining
Battery Cells
Service & Collision
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

A single-column header with the title left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 4.4% from left, title baseline near 14% of height). Below it, a large light-grey rounded panel spans roughly 4.5% to 95.6% horizontally and 19.4% to 89.5% vertically, holding a 3-row by 5-column grid of icons (rows at about 25–36%, 49–60% and 70–82% of height; columns centred near 13%, 31%, 50%, 69%, 90%). Icons sit centred above their centred labels, with directional grey arrows linking them. A Tesla wordmark sits bottom-left at about 4.4% from left and 94.3% from top, with the copyright line beside it; there is no slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram of a Tesla energy and vehicle ecosystem with 14 product images and connecting grey arrows. Top row, left to right: Autobidder (a desktop monitor showing a dark data graph) with a double-headed diagonal arrow to Energy Storage; Optimus (a humanoid robot, standing, dark metal and grey) with a left-pointing arrow from AI Compute; AI Compute (a copper-coloured square computer module with a perforated top) with a right-pointing arrow to Full Self-Driving; Full Self-Driving (a white Model Y-style car seen from above on a road with a blue sensor beam, faded edges) with a double-headed arrow to Robotaxi; Robotaxi (a black smartphone showing a dark app screen). Middle row: Solar (a dark grey solar panel, vertical) with a right-pointing arrow to Energy Storage; Energy Storage (a white Powerwall-style wall battery) with a right-pointing arrow to Charging; Charging (a white Tesla wall connector with a black cable) with a right-pointing arrow to Vehicles; Vehicles (a red Model Y, side view, three-quarter angle) with a double-headed vertical arrow up to Full Self-Driving and a diagonal double-headed arrow to Robotaxi; Insurance (a phone showing an app with a blue gauge reading 90 and a blue button) with a left-pointing arrow to Vehicles. Bottom row: Lithium Refining (a grey industrial refining plant with stacks) with a right-pointing arrow to Battery Cells; Battery Cells (a silver cylindrical cell, upright) with a diagonal arrow up-left to Energy Storage and a diagonal arrow up-right to Vehicles; Service & Collision (a grey Model Y with two people standing and kneeling beside it) with an upward arrow to Vehicles. Arrowheads are small open chevrons. Photographs are rendered as product cut-outs on a light grey background with soft shadows; none are cropped to shape. Lighting is soft, studio-style and even, with dominant colours grey, white, red and copper.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a very light grey #F5F6F8 rounded panel behind the diagram. Clean minimal product-diagram slide on a white field. Title in a bold geometric grotesque sans-serif (UniversalSansDisplay Medium), black #000000, very large (about 5.9% of slide height per the measured size). Labels in UniversalSansDisplay Regular, near-black #111111, about 1.85% of slide height. Copyright in regular sans, light grey #ACACB0, about 1.3%. Panel fill very light grey #F5F6F8 with generous rounded corners (about 1% radius) and no shadow or border. Connector arrows are thin mid-grey lines (#B3B3B3, roughly 1 px) with small open arrowheads. Accent colour comes only from the product images (red car, blue sensor beam and gauge).

### Slide 25

**Text**

No Longer Compute Constrained / We're making necessary investments in AI compute infrastructure for our ambitious product roadmap / 40k / 35k / 30k / 25k / 20k / 15k / 10k / 5k / 0 / Sept 2019 / March 2020 / Sept 2020 / March 2021 / Sept 2021 / March 2022 / Sept 2022 / March 2023 / Sept 2023 / March 2024 / Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle stacked in the top-left, starting at about 4.4% from the left edge. A large rounded light-grey chart panel spans the content width (about 4.5% to 95.6% horizontally, 19.4% to 89.5% vertically). The Tesla wordmark sits bottom-left at about 4.4% from the left with the copyright line beside it at about 16.9%. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

One area chart (filled line chart) inside the grey panel. Title: none printed on the chart. Y-axis: 0 to 40k, gridlines and labels every 5k (0, 5k, 10k, 15k, 20k, 25k, 30k, 35k, 40k), no unit label. X-axis: labelled Sept 2019, March 2020, Sept 2020, March 2021, Sept 2021, March 2022, Sept 2022, March 2023, Sept 2023, March 2024, spaced about every 6 months. Single series, a stepped line in medium blue #4169E1 with a light blue #B8C9F8 fill down to zero; no values are printed, so values are estimates: Sept 2019 about 200, March 2020 about 400, about 900 by mid 2020, about 1,000 Sept 2020, about 1,500 late 2020, about 2,000 March 2021, about 2,700 mid 2021, about 3,300 Sept 2021 to March 2022, about 3,800 from spring 2022 to March 2023, about 4,000 to 5,000 in 2023, about 5,400 mid 2023, about 7,000 Sept 2023, about 15,200 Oct 2023, about 16,000 Nov 2023 to Jan 2024, about 19,000 Feb 2024, about 38,000 March 2024. No legend, no highlighted points; the final point at March 2024 is the highest and sits at the right edge of the plot.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background. White background with a very light grey rounded panel #F6F7F8 behind the chart. Title in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (Universal Sans Text Medium, looks like Helvetica-style), black #000000. Subtitle in light-weight sans-serif, grey #989898. Axis labels in regular sans-serif, grey #8D8D8D. Copyright in light grey #ACACB0. Horizontal gridlines are thin light grey #D0D0D0 lines. Title text height about 3.7% of slide height; subtitle about 2.55%; axis labels about 2.04%; copyright about 1.3%. Panel corners are rounded with a small radius, no shadow, no gradient.

### Slide 26

**Text**

FSD (Supervised) V12 Wide Release / FSD (Supervised) does not make your car autonomous. Always pay attention to the road and be prepared to take over immediately. / T E S L A / Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the slide with the title in the upper left at about 8.4% from the top and the disclaimer line near the bottom at about 88.3%. A footer row at about 94.3% holds the Tesla wordmark at left and the copyright line beside it, both starting at a left margin of about 4.4%. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of the interior of a dark Tesla car as seen from the driver's seat. The steering wheel with the Tesla T logo occupies the lower left, and the centre touchscreen shows a top-down driving visualisation of the car: a blue car shape with a yellow outline beside grey road and parked-vehicle shapes. Through the windshield is a sunny residential street sloping away, with a white Ford Focus-style hatchback and a row of parked cars on the left, a grey Jeep Grand Cherokee with lit red tail lamps directly ahead, a utility pole on the right, yellow and cream houses, hedges, a blue wheelie bin and trees. The driver's hands rest on the wheel and lower centre console. Composition is a wide, centred one-point perspective from the driver's seat. Lighting is bright daylight through the windshield with a darker, shadowed cabin. Dominant colours are black, charcoal and wood-grain brown in the cabin, with grey asphalt, pastel yellow and cream houses and green foliage outside. The photograph is full-bleed, uncropped at the edges, with a dark translucent tint over the whole image to lower contrast behind the text, and a darker band across the top. A small Tesla wordmark logo sits in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of the car interior on top. Black background with a full-bleed photograph and a dark overlay. The title is white medium-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (Universal Sans Display Medium), about 5.9% of slide height, sentence-case and left aligned. The disclaimer is grey #848484 regular sans-serif about 1.85% of slide height. The copyright is light grey #ACACB0 regular sans-serif about 1.3% of slide height. The Tesla wordmark is widely letter-spaced capitals in light grey. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or gradients beyond the top darkening band and the overall tint.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Coming Soon
Copyright 2024 Tesla Inc.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph with the title 'Coming Soon' at top left and a footer row at bottom left: the Tesla wordmark logo followed by the copyright line. Left margin is about 4.4% of slide width. No slide number and no grid is visible.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph: a close-up of a woman's hands holding a black smartphone in a dark rounded protective case. The phone is angled diagonally from upper left to lower right and fills the centre of the frame. The screen is black and shows a pinkish-red glowing Tesla wordmark at top centre, the time '6:57' at top left, a small signal icon and battery icon at top right, a circular arc graphic with a thin dashed inner circle, small dots and triangle marks around it, and the word 'SUMMON' in widely letterspaced capitals in the centre. A thin white horizontal home-indicator bar sits at lower right of the screen. The hands are lit softly from the front; the background is an out-of-focus beige and grey car interior with a blue-grey tint at right and a warm brown area at lower left. The photo is cropped to full bleed with no border. A dark gradient overlay darkens the top band of the slide, behind the title. Footer: a small grey Tesla wordmark logo, about 30% of slide width, at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of hands holding a phone over a blurred car interior, on a #000000 fill, with a dark gradient across the top band. Black background (#000000) with a full-bleed photograph over it. Title is white (#FFFFFF) Universal Sans Display Medium, sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height. Footer copyright is grey (#ACACB0) Universal Sans Display Regular, about 1.3% of slide height. Footer logo is grey (#ACACB0). Phone screen glow is pink-red (about #E8787F). No shapes, borders, corner radii or shadows apart from the phone's own rounded case. The top gradient overlay fades from dark at the top edge into the photo.
